    function pulseRuntimeState(stateId, mode = "enter", portSide = null) {
      const id = String(stateId || "");
      if (!id) return;
      const pulseClass = runtimeStatePulseClass(mode);
      const token = String(++runtimePulseSerial);
      if (!(activeRuntimeStatePulses instanceof Map)) activeRuntimeStatePulses = new Map();
      const started = performance.now();
      const key = runtimeStatePulseKey(id, mode, portSide);
      activeRuntimeStatePulses.set(key, {
        id,
        mode,
        portSide,
        pulseClass,
        token,
        started,
        until: started + runtimeStatePulseDuration(mode)
      });
      applyRuntimeStatePulses(started);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => applyRuntimeStatePulses());
      setTimeout(() => {
        const pulse = activeRuntimeStatePulses.get(key);
        if (pulse?.token !== token) return;
        activeRuntimeStatePulses.delete(key);
        cleanupRuntimeStatePulse(id, token, pulseClass, portSide);
      }, runtimeStatePulseDuration(mode) + 80);
    }

    function hostAcceptsRuntimeMessage(evt, data) {

        data &&

    }

ion navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html) {
      const url = createStandaloneAppUrlFromHtml(html);
      if (popup) {
        try { popup.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
        try {
          if (popup.location?.replace) popup.location.replace(url);
          else popup.location.href = url;
          try { popup.focus(); } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      try {
        const opened = window.open(url, "_blank");
        if (opened) {
          try { opened.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
      revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url);
      return false;
    }

    function openStandaloneAppWindow() {
      let popup = null;
      try { popup = window.open("about:blank", "_blank"); } catch (_) {}
      let html = "";
      try {
        html = standaloneAppHtml();
      } catch (err) {
        try { popup?.close?.(); } catch (_) {}
        modalAlert(err?.message || "Die App konnte nicht in einem neuen Fenster vorbereitet werden.", "Öffnen fehlgeschlagen");
        return false;
      }
      if (writeStandaloneAppToPopup(popup, html)) return true;
      if (navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html)) return true;
      modalAlert("Der Browser hat das neue Fenster blockiert. Bitte Pop-ups für diese Seite erlauben und erneut tippen.", "Öffnen blockiert");
      return false;
    }

    function revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url) {
      const blobUrl = String(url || "").split("#", 1)[0];
      if (blobUrl && blobUrl !== APP_URL) URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    }

    function runtimePayloadForSession(payload, sessionId) {
      return { ...payload, sessionId };

    function postRuntimePayloadToWindow(targetWindow, payload, sessionId) {
      if (!targetWindow || !sessionId || !payload || typeof payload !== "object") return false;
      targetWindow.postMessage(runtimePayloadForSession(payload, sessionId), RUNTIME_MESSAGE_ORIGIN);
      return true;
    }

    function runtimeSyncAtOrAfter(value, expected) {
      const actualNumber = Number(value);
      const expectedNumber = Number(expected);
      if (Number.isSafeInteger(actualNumber) && Number.isSafeInteger(expectedNumber)) {
        return actualNumber >= expectedNumber;
      }
      return String(value || "") === String(expected || "");
    }

    function queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      const type = String(payload.type || "");
      if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      } else if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      }
      pendingFramePayloads.push(payload);
    }

    function pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(payload) {
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return 0;
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") return 1;
      return 2;
    }

    function flushPendingRuntimePayloads() {
      if (!appFrameReady || !frameEl.contentWindow || !appFrameContainsGeneratedApp()) return false;
      const queue = pendingFramePayloads
        .map((payload, index) => ({ payload, index }))
        .sort((a, b) => pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(a.payload) - pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(b.payload) || a.index - b.index)
        .map(item => item.payload);
      pendingFramePayloads = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index += 1) {
        try {
          postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, queue[index], appFrameSessionId);
        } catch (_) {
          pendingFramePayloads.unshift(...queue.slice(index));
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    }

    function runtimeModelPayloadForReload() {
      if (!productContract) return null;
      const activeStateId = hostRuntimeStateView();
      const startStateId = activeStateId && byId(activeStateId) ? activeStateId : model.initial;
      return {
        type: "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL",
        model: snapshotModelForRuntime(),
        reset: true,
        startStateId,
        paused: hostRuntimePausedView(),
        preserveFocus: true
      };
    }

    function reloadAppFrame(payload = null, opts = {}) {
      const reloadPayload = runtimeModelPayloadForReload();
      if (payload?.type !== "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL" && reloadPayload) queuePendingRuntimePayload(reloadPayload);
      if (payload) queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
      const activeStateId = hostRuntimeStateView();
      if (!payload && opts.preserveCurrent && activeStateId && byId(activeStateId)) {
        pendingAppStartStateId = activeStateId;
        pendingAppStartSuppressLayerFollow = false;
      }
      appFrameReady = false;
      const previousUrl = appFrameUrl;
      appFrameSessionId = createRuntimeSessionId();
      appFrameUrl = createRuntimeAppUrl(appFrameSessionId);
      frameEl.src = appFrameUrl;
      revokeRuntimeAppUrl(previousUrl);
    }

    function postRuntimePayload(payload) {
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      if (appFrameReady && frameEl.contentWindow) {
        if (!appFrameContainsGeneratedApp()) {
          reloadAppFrame(payload);
        } else {
          try {
            postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, payload, appFrameSessionId);
          } catch (_) {
            queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
          }
        }
      } else {
        queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload);
      }
      return payload;
    }

        startStateId: instance.root.id,
        paused: false,
        preserveFocus: true
      };
    }

    function unbindPresetInfoPreviewDismiss() {
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePresetInfoPreviewOutside, true);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handlePresetInfoPreviewKeydown, true);
      window.removeEventListener("resize", closePresetInfoOverlay);
      window.removeEventListener("scroll", closePresetInfoOverlay, true);
    }

    function closePresetInfoOverlay() {
      if (presetInfoPreviewSendTimer) {
        clearTimeout(presetInfoPreviewSendTimer);
        presetInfoPreviewSendTimer = null;
      }
      unbindPresetInfoPreviewDismiss();
      presetInfoPreviewAnchor?.querySelector?.(".template-info")?.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false");
      presetInfoPreviewAnchor?.classList?.remove("info-open");
      presetInfoPreviewAnchor = null;
      if (presetInfoPreviewEl) {
        presetInfoPreviewEl.remove();
        presetInfoPreviewEl = null;
      }
      if (presetInfoPreviewUrl) {
        revokeRuntimeAppUrl(presetInfoPreviewUrl);
        presetInfoPreviewUrl = "";
      }
    }

    function handlePresetInfoPreviewOutside(evt) {
      if (!presetInfoPreviewEl) return;
      if (presetInfoPreviewEl.contains(evt.target)) return;
      if (presetInfoPreviewAnchor?.contains?.(evt.target)) return;
      closePresetInfoOverlay();
    }

    function handlePresetInfoPreviewKeydown(evt) {
      if (evt.key === "Escape") closePresetInfoOverlay();
    }

    function positionPresetInfoOverlay(anchor, overlay) {
      if (!anchor || !overlay) return;
      const margin = 10;
      const viewportW = window.innerWidth || document.documentElement.clientWidth || 1024;
      const viewportH = window.innerHeight || document.documentElement.clientHeight || 768;
      const mobile = isMobileWorkspaceLayout();
      const width = Math.min(mobile ? 420 : 380, Math.max(260, viewportW - margin * 2));
      overlay.style.width = width + "px";
      const rect = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
      const height = Math.min(overlay.offsetHeight || 420, Math.max(260, viewportH - margin * 2));
      const left = mobile
        ? clamp((viewportW - width) / 2, margin, viewportW - width - margin)
        : clamp(rect.left, margin, viewportW - width - margin);
      const below = rect.bottom + margin;
      const above = rect.top - height - margin;
      const top = mobile
        ? clamp(rect.top + rect.height / 2 - height / 2, margin, viewportH - height - margin)
        : above >= margin
          ? above
          : clamp(below, margin, viewportH - height - margin);
      overlay.style.left = left +

etPreviewPopover";
      overlay.className = "preset-preview-popover";

        <div class="preset-preview-head">
          <div class="preset-preview-title"></div>
          <button class="preset-preview-close" id="presetPreviewClose" type="button" aria-label="Vorschau schließen" title="Vorschau schließen"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M18 6 6 18"></path><path d="m6 6 12 12"></path></svg></button>
          <div class="preset-preview-desc"></div>
        </div>
        <div class="preset-preview-frame-wrap">
          <iframe class="preset-preview-frame" title="${escapeHtml(title)} Vorschau"></iframe>
        </div>
      `;
      overlay.querySelector(".preset-preview-title").textContent = title;
      overlay.querySelector(".preset-preview-desc").textContent = description;
      overlay.querySelector(".preset-preview-close").onclick = evt => {
        evt.preventDefault();
        evt.stopPropagation();

        window.addEventListener("resize", closePresetInfoOverlay);

    function syncToApp(reset, opts = {}) {
      if (!productContract) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return null;
      clearPresetPreviewUi();
      syncStateEl.textContent = reset ? "neu" : "aktiv";
      if (reset) {
        pendingHistoryRestoreSyncId = "";
        pendingAppStartStateId = null;
        pendingAppStartSuppressLayerFollow = false;
        pendingRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds.clear();
      }
      const defaultOverrideStateIds = reset ? [] : takeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(opts.defaultOverrideStateIds);
      const payload = {
        type: "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL",
        model: snapshotModelForRuntime(),
        reset: Boolean(reset),
        paused: hostRuntimePausedView(),
        preserveFocus: !reset
      };
      if (defaultOverrideStateIds.length) payload.defaultOverrideStateIds = defaultOverrideStateIds;
      const sentPayload = postRuntimePayload(payload);
      setTimeout(() => { syncStateEl.textContent = "aktiv"; }, 400);
      return sentPayload;
    }

    function syncRuntimeAfterStateDataChange(state, opts = {}) {
      if (!state?.id) {
        scheduleSync(false);
        return;
      }
      const defaultOverrideStateIds = opts.defaultOverride ? [state.id] : [];
      const runtimeStateId = hostRuntimeStateView();
      const activeState = runtimeStateId ? byId(runtimeStateId) : null;
      const affectsActiveView = runtimeStateId === state.id || Boolean(activeState && isDescendant(state.id, runtimeStateId));
      if (affectsActiveView && opts.immediate !== false) {
        if (defaultOverrideStateIds.length) syncToApp(false, { defaultOverrideStateIds });
        else syncToApp(false);
      } else {
        if (defaultOverrideStateIds.length) scheduleSync(false, { defaultOverrideStateIds });
        else scheduleSync(false);
      }
    }

    function startAppAtState(id, opts = {}) {
      if (!productContract) return;
      if (!byId(id)) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return;
      clearPresetPreviewUi();
      pendingHistoryRestoreSyncId = "";
      runtimeLayerFollowEnabled = Boolean(opts.allowLayerFollow);
      if (opts.allowLayerFollow) suppressRuntimeLayerFollowUntil = 0;
      if (opts.suppressLayerFollow) suppressRuntimeLayerFollowUntil = Date.now() + 4000;
      pendingAppStartStateId = id;
      pendingAppStartSuppressLayerFollow = Boolean(opts.suppressLayerFollow);
      syncStateEl.textContent = "Start";
      const payload = {
        type: "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL",
        model: snapshotModelForRuntime(),
        reset: Boolean(opts.resetContext),
        startStateId: id,
        paused: hostRuntimePausedView(),
        preserveFocus: Boolean(opts.preserveFocus)
      };
      const defaultOverrideStateIds = opts.resetContext ? [] : takeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(opts.defaultOverrideStateIds);
      if (defaultOverrideStateIds.length) payload.defaultOverrideStateIds = defaultOverrideStateIds;
      postRuntimePayload(payload);
      if (opts.redraw !== false) draw();
      setTimeout(() => { syncStateEl.textContent = "aktiv"; }, 400);
    }

    function safeExternalAppUrl(url) {
      const raw = String(url || "").trim();
      if (!raw) return "";
      try {
        const parsed = new URL(raw);
        return parsed.protocol === "http:" || parsed.protocol === "https:" ? parsed.href : "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    function openPreviewExternalUrl(url) {
      const href = safeExternalAppUrl(url);
      if (!href) return;
      try { window.open(href, "_blank", "noopener,noreferrer"); } catch (_) {}
    }

    function nextStateTitle() {
      let i = model.states.length + 1;
      const titles = new Set(model.states.map(s => String(s.title || "").toLowerCase()));
      while (titles.has(("Zustand " + i).toLowerCase())) i++;
      return "Zustand " + i;
    }

    function makeState(x, y, title, parentId = currentLayerId) {
      title = title || nextStateTitle();
      const id = uniqueModelEntityId(title);
      const state = {
        id,
        title,
        components: [],
        data: {},
        dataTypes: {},
        dataSource: normalizeDataSource({ target: stateDataScopeForId(id) + ".fetch" }),
        repeat: normalizeRepeatConfig(null),
        dataWires: [],
        boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(null),
        parentId: parentId && byId(parentId) ? parentId : null,
        x,
        y
      };
      state.x = snapClampToGrid(x, WORLD_MIN_X, WORLD_MAX_X - nodeWidth(state));
      state.y = snapClampToGrid(y, WORLD_MIN_Y, WORLD_MAX_Y - NODE_H);
      return state;
    }

    function simpleContentPresetComponent(template) {
      if (!template?.builtIn) return null;
      const copy = snapshotStateTemplates([template])[0];
      if (!copy) return null;
      const components = normalizeComponents(copy.components);
      if (components.length !== 1 || !componentPresetTypes().includes(components[0].type)) return null;
      if (snapshotTemplateStates(copy).length || snapshotTemplateTransitions(copy).length) return null;
      if (normalizeDataWires(copy.dataWires).length) return null;
      return cloneComponents(components)[0] || null;
    }

    function primaryEditableComponentId(state) {
      const component = normalizeComponents(state?.components).find(component =>
        ["heading", "text", "image", "link", "note", "list", "daisy"].includes(component.type)
      );
      return component?.id || "";
    }

    function openPrimaryComponentEditor(state) {
      const componentId = primaryEditableComponentId(state);
      if (componentId) openComponentEditorIds.add(componentId);
      return componentId;
    }

    function addSimpleContentPresetToState(state, template, historyKey) {
      const component = simpleContentPresetComponent(template);
      if (!state || state.virtualProxy || !component) return false;
      component.id = uid("c");
      state.components = [...normalizeComponents(state.components), component];
      openComponentEditorIds.add(component.id);
      selected = selectionFromParts([state.id], []);
      saveModel(historyKey || ("state:component:add:" + state.id));
      draw();
      showNodeInspector(state, { focus: false });
      syncRuntimeAfterStateDataChange(state, { resetActive: false });
      return true;
    }

    function createRuntimeSessionId() {
      const bytes = new Uint8Array(16);
      window.crypto.getRandomValues(bytes);
      return [...bytes].map(value => value.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
    }

    function runtimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const url = new URL(APP_URL);
      const hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId });
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room)) hash.set("room", room);
      url.hash = hash.toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function createRuntimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([GENERATED_APP_HTML], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      const hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId });
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      if (/^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room)) hash.set("room", room);
      url.hash = hash.toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function createDetachedRuntimeAppUrl(sessionId) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([GENERATED_APP_HTML], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      url.hash = new URLSearchParams({ [RUNTIME_SESSION_FRAGMENT_KEY]: sessionId }).toString();
      return url.href;
    }

    function standaloneAppHtml() {
      return buildStandaloneAppHtml(GENERATED_APP_HTML, definitionPayload());
    }

    function standaloneRoomFragment() {
      const room = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("room") || "";
      return /^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}$/.test(room) ? new URLSearchParams({ room }).toString() : "";
    }

    function createStandaloneAppUrlFromHtml(html) {
      const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([html], { type: "text/html" }));
      const url = new URL(blobUrl);
      const roomFragment = standaloneRoomFragment();
      if (roomFragment) url.hash = roomFragment;
      return url.href;
    }


    function writeStandaloneAppToPopup(popup, html) {
      if (!popup || !popup.document) return false;
      try {
        const roomFragment = standaloneRoomFragment();
        if (roomFragment) popup.location.hash = roomFragment;
        popup.document.open();
        popup.document.write(html);
        popup.document.close();
        try { popup.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
        try { popup.focus(); } catch (_) {}
        return true;
      } catch (_) {
        return false;
      }
    }

    function navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html) {
      const url = createStandaloneAppUrlFromHtml(html);
      if (popup) {
        try { popup.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
        try {
          if (popup.location?.replace) popup.location.replace(url);
          else popup.location.href = url;
          try { popup.focus(); } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        } catch (_) {}
      }
      try {
        const opened = window.open(url, "_blank");
        if (opened) {
          try { opened.opener = null; } catch (_) {}
          setTimeout(() => revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url), 60000);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (_) {}
      revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url);
      return false;
    }

    function openStandaloneAppWindow() {
      let popup = null;
      try { popup = window.open("about:blank", "_blank"); } catch (_) {}
      let html = "";
      try {
        html = standaloneAppHtml();
      } catch (err) {
        try { popup?.close?.(); } catch (_) {}
        modalAlert(err?.message || "Die App konnte nicht in einem neuen Fenster vorbereitet werden.", "Öffnen fehlgeschlagen");
        return false;
      }
      if (writeStandaloneAppToPopup(popup, html)) return true;
      if (navigatePopupToStandaloneUrl(popup, html)) return true;
      modalAlert("Der Browser hat das neue Fenster blockiert. Bitte Pop-ups für diese Seite erlauben und erneut tippen.", "Öffnen blockiert");
      return false;
    }

    function revokeRuntimeAppUrl(url) {
      const blobUrl = String(url || "").split("#", 1)[0];
      if (blobUrl && blobUrl !== APP_URL) URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl);
    }

    function runtimePayloadForSession(payload, sessionId) {
      return { ...payload, sessionId };
    }

    function postRuntimePayloadToWindow(targetWindow, payload, sessionId) {
      if (!targetWindow || !sessionId || !payload || typeof payload !== "object") return false;
      targetWindow.postMessage(runtimePayloadForSession(payload, sessionId), RUNTIME_MESSAGE_ORIGIN);
      return true;
    }

    function isSingleEmptyInitialStateModel(candidate) {
      if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") return false;
      const states = Array.isArray(candidate.states) ? candidate.states : [];
      const transitions = Array.isArray(candidate.transitions) ? candidate.transitions : [];
      if (states.length !== 1 || transitions.length) return false;
      const state = states[0] || {};
      const stateId = String(state.id || "").trim();
      const initial = String(candidate.initial || "").trim();
      if (!stateId || initial !== stateId) return false;
      if (String(state.parentId || "").trim()) return false;
      if (Array.isArray(state.components) && state.components.length) return false;
      if (state.data && typeof state.data === "object" && Object.keys(state.data).length) return false;
      if (state.dataTypes && typeof state.dataTypes === "object" && Object.keys(state.dataTypes).length) return false;
      if (Array.isArray(state.dataWires) && state.dataWires.length) return false;
      if (Array.isArray(state.subscriptions) && state.subscriptions.length) return false;
      return true;
    }

    function modelSnapshotValue() {
      normalizeModel(model);
      const issue = modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model);
      if (issue) throw new Error(issue.message);
      const snapshot = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(model));
      if (dragging?.moved) {
        const draggedState = snapshot.states.find(state => state.id === dragging.id);
        if (draggedState) {
          draggedState.x = dragging.originalX;
          draggedState.y = dragging.originalY;
        }
      }
      return snapshot;
    }

    function modelDefinitionValue(value) {
      const normalized = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value)));
      return {
        version: DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        name: String(normalized.name || "Unbenannter Ablauf"),
        initial: String(normalized.initial || ""),
        boundary: normalizeBoundaryConfig(normalized.boundary),
        states: normalized.states.map(stateExportSnapshot),
        transitions: normalized.transitions
          .filter(transition => !isBoundaryFlowTransition(transition))
          .map(transitionExportSnapshot)
      };
    }

    function modelDefinitionSnapshot() {
      return modelDefinitionValue(modelSnapshotValue());
    }

    function normalizeSelection(value = selected) {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
      if ("nodes" in value || "edges" in value) return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges, { allCurrentLayer: value.allCurrentLayer === true });
      if (value.type === "node") return selectionFromParts([value.id], []);
      if (value.type === "edge") return selectionFromParts([], [value.id]);
      if (value.type === "nodes") return selectionFromParts(value.ids, []);
      if (value.type === "items") return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges);
      return null;
    }

    function parseEditorSnapshot(snapshot) {
      const data = JSON.parse(snapshot);
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) throw new Error("Invalid editor history snapshot.");
      return {
        model: data.model,
        selected: data.selected || null,
        currentLayerId: data.currentLayerId || null
      };
    }

    function persistModel(sync = true) {
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return false;
      try {
        localStorage.setItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY, editorStorageSnapshot());
      } catch (_) {}
      if (sync) scheduleSync(false);
      return true;
    }

    function cancelInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
        pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = null;
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = null;
    }

    function flushInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (!pendingInspectorTextCommit) return true;
      const pending = pendingInspectorTextCommit;
      cancelInspectorTextCommit();
      flushingInspectorTextCommit = true;
      let saved = false;
      try {
        saved = saveEditorState(pending.historyKey, pending.sync);
      } finally {
        flushingInspectorTextCommit = false;
      }
      if (saved && pending.draw) scheduleDraw();
      return saved;
    }

    function scheduleInspectorTextCommit(historyKey, opts = {}) {
      const next = {
        historyKey,
        sync: opts.sync !== false,
        draw: Boolean(opts.draw)
      };
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit.historyKey !== historyKey) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = pendingInspectorTextCommit
        ? {
            historyKey,
            sync: pendingInspectorTextCommit.sync || next.sync,
            draw: pendingInspectorTextCommit.draw || next.draw
          }
        : next;
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
      pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = setTimeout(flushInspectorTextCommit, opts.delay ?? 120);
    }

    function updateHistoryButtons() {

      const canRedo = Boolean(redoStack.length);
      if (btnCanvasUndo) btnCanvasUndo.disabled = !canUndo;
      if (btnCanvasRedo) btnCanvasRedo.disabled = !canRedo;
    }

    function undoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!undoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();

    }

    function isPlainObject(value) {
      return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
    }

    function assertPlainObject(value, path) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an object.`);
    }

    function assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error(`${path} must be a string.`);
      if (!allowEmpty && !value.trim()) throw new Error(`${path} must not be empty.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertFiniteNumber(value, path) {
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertNoNullish(value, path = "definition") {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) throw new Error(`${path} must not be null or undefined.`);
      if (typeof value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      if (!["boolean", "number", "object", "string"].includes(typeof value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a JSON value.`);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, index)) throw new Error(`${path}[${index}] must not be an array hole.`);
          assertNoNullish(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (typeof value !== "object") return;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a plain JSON object.`);
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        assertNoNullish(item, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateNullableString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
      return assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

    function validateComponents(value, path, { stateId = "" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an array.`);
      return value.map((component, index) => {
        const itemPath = `${path}[${index}]`;
        assertPlainObject(component, itemPath);
        const type = assertString(component.type, `${itemPath}.type`);
        if (!["heading", "text", "image", "list", "link", "note", "divider", "daisy", "transitionButton", "dataWire"].includes(type)) throw new Error(`${itemPath}.type is not supported.`);
        const normalized = {
          id: assertString(component.id, `${itemPath}.id`),
          type,
          text: validateComponentLiteralText(component.text, `${itemPath}.text`),
          url: "url" in component ? validateComponentLiteralText(component.url, `${itemPath}.url`) : "",
          variant: "variant" in component ? assertString(component.variant, `${itemPath}.variant`, { allowEmpty: true }) : ""
        };
        if ("dataPath" in component) normalized.dataPath = validateComponentDataPath(component.dataPath, `${itemPath}.dataPath`, { allowEmpty: true, stateId });
        if ("dataRole" in component) normalized.dataRole = assertString(component.dataRole, `${itemPath}.dataRole`, { allowEmpty: true }).slice(0, 40);
        if ("dataLabel" in component) normalized.dataLabel = assertString(component.dataLabel, `${itemPath}.dataLabel`, { allowEmpty: true }).slice(0, 80);
        if (type === "transitionButton") normalized.transitionId = assertString(component.transitionId || "", `${itemPath}.transitionId`, { allowEmpty: true });
        if (type === "dataWire") normalized.wireId = assertString(component.wireId || "", `${itemPath}.wireId`, { allowEmpty: true });

      if (value === null || value === undefined) throw new Error(`${path} must not be null or undefined.`);
      if (typeof value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      if (!["boolean", "number", "object", "string"].includes(typeof value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a JSON value.`);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, index)) throw new Error(`${path}[${index}] must not be an array hole.`);
          assertNoNullish(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (typeof value !== "object") return;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a plain JSON object.`);
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        assertNoNullish(item, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateNullableString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
      return assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

    function validateComponents(value, path, { stateId = "" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an array.`);
      return value.map((component, index) => {
        const itemPath = `${path}[${index}]`;
        assertPlainObject(component, itemPath);
        const type = assertString(component.type, `${itemPath}.type`);
        if (!["heading", "text", "image", "list", "link", "note", "divider", "daisy", "transitionButton", "dataWire"].includes(type)) throw new Error(`${itemPath}.type is not supported.`);
        const normalized = {
          id: assertString(component.id, `${itemPath}.id`),
          type,
          text: validateComponentLiteralText(component.text, `${itemPath}.text`),
          url: "url" in component ? validateComponentLiteralText(component.url, `${itemPath}.url`) : "",
          variant: "variant" in component ? assertString(component.variant, `${itemPath}.variant`, { allowEmpty: true }) : ""
        };
        if ("dataPath" in component) normalized.dataPath = validateComponentDataPath(component.dataPath, `${itemPath}.dataPath`, { allowEmpty: true, stateId });
        if ("dataRole" in component) normalized.dataRole = assertString(component.dataRole, `${itemPath}.dataRole`, { allowEmpty: true }).slice(0, 40);
        if ("dataLabel" in component) normalized.dataLabel = assertString(component.dataLabel, `${itemPath}.dataLabel`, { allowEmpty: true }).slice(0, 80);
        if (type === "transitionButton") normalized.transitionId = assertString(component.transitionId || "", `${itemPath}.transitionId`, { allowEmpty: true });
        if (type === "dataWire") normalized.wireId = assertString(component.wireId || "", `${itemPath}.wireId`, { allowEmpty: true });
        if (type === "list") {
          normalized.items = normalizeListItems(component.items, normalized.text);
          normalized.items.forEach((item, itemIndex) => {
            item.text = validateComponentLiteralText(item.text, `${itemPath}.items[${itemIndex}].text`);
            item.url = validateComponentLiteralText(item.url, `${itemPath}.items[${itemIndex}].url`);
          });
        }
        return normalized;
      });
    }

    function validateDataObject(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return {};
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      assertNoNullish(value, path);
      return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(value));
    }

    function validateStateDataValue(value, path) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        value.forEach((item, index) => validateStateDataValue(item, `${path}[${index}]`));
        return;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return;
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        if (!/^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*$/.test(key)) {
          throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must use a local identifier without dots.`);
        }
        validateStateDataValue(child, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateStateDataObject(value, path) {
      const data = valid

===== definition @ 270156 =====
    function transitionTriggerTypeIsContractual(transition) {
      return TRANSITION_TRIGGER_CONTRACT_TYPES.has(transitionTriggerContractType(transition));
    }

    function transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition) {
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
      if (triggerType === "flow") return "";
      if (triggerType === "button") return `button:${transition.id}`;
      if (triggerType === "auto" || triggerType === "timer") return triggerType;
      const eventName = normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent(transition);
      return triggerType === "change"
        ? eventName ? `change:${eventName}` : ""
        : eventName ? `${triggerType}:${eventName}` : "";
    }

    function transitionTriggerContractKey(transition) {
      const baseKey = transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);
      if (!baseKey) return "";
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
      if (!["change", "event", "realtime", "api"].includes(triggerType)) return baseKey;
      const matchKey = canonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition?.triggerMatch);
      return matchKey ? `${baseKey}|match:${matchKey}` : "";
    }

    function validateTransitionTriggerContracts(transitions) {
      const byState = new Map();
      for (const transition of transitions) {
        const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
        if (triggerType === "flow") continue;
        const stateId = effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(transition);
        if (!byState.has(stateId)) byState.set(stateId, []);
        byState.get(stateId).push(transition);
      }
      for (const [stateId, outgoing] of byState) {
        if (outgoing.some(transition => normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) === "auto") && outgoing.length !== 1) {
          throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions from ${stateId} must contain only one auto transition and no other trigger.`);
        }
        const claims = new Map();
        const eventClaims = new Map();
        for (const transition of outgoing) {
          validateTransitionTriggerMatchContract(transition, `definition.model.transitions.${transition.id}.triggerMatch`);
          const key = transitionTriggerContractKey(transition);
          if (!key) throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} must define one concrete trigger.`);
          const baseKey = transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition);
          const matchKey = canonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition.triggerMatch);
          if (claims.has(key)) {
            throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} duplicates trigger ${key} from ${stateId}.`);
          }
          claims.set(key, transition.id);
          if (baseKey && ["change", "event", "realtime", "api"].includes(normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition))) {
            const existing = eventClaims.get(baseKey) || [];
            const hasCatchAll = existing.some(item => item.matchKey === "*");
            if ((matchKey === "*" && existing.length) || (matchKey !== "*" && hasCatchAll)) {
              throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} conflicts with catch-all trigger ${baseKey} from ${stateId}.`);
            }
            const overlapping = matchKey !== "*" ? existing.find(item => transitionTriggerMatchesCanOverlap(item.transition, transition)) : null;
            if (overlapping) {
              throw new Error(`definition.model.transitions.${transition.id} overlaps trigger match ${baseKey} from ${stateId}.`);
            }
            existing.push({ id: transition.id, matchKey, transition });
            eventClaims.set(baseKey, existing);
          }
        }
      }
    }

    function validateComponentDataPath(value, path, { allowEmpty = false, stateId = "" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const text = assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty }).trim();
      if (!text) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const clean = validateBindingPath(text, path, { allowEmpty });
      if (!clean) return "";
      const ownerScope = stateId ? stateDataScopeForId(stateId) : "";
      if (ownerScope && clean !== ownerScope && !clean.startsWith(ownerScope + ".")) {
        throw new Error(`${path} must bind data owned by states.${stateId}.`);
      }
      if (!ownerScope && !/^states\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.|$)/.test(clean)) {
        throw new Error(`${path} must be a fully qualified states.<id> data scope.`);
      }
      return clean;
    }

    function validateDataTypePath(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      const text = assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty }).trim();
      if (!text) return allowEmpty ? "" : "";
      return validateBindingPath(text, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

    function validateDataWires(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an array.`);
      return value.slice(0, 80).map((wire, index) => {
        const itemPath = `${path}[${index}]`;
        assertPlainObject(wire, itemPath);
        const sourcePath = validateReadableBusPath(wire.sourcePath || wire.path, `${itemPath}.sourcePath`);
        const role = normalizeDataWireRole(wire.role, "field");
        return {
          id: assertString(wire.id, `${itemPath}.id`),
          sourcePath,
          scopePath: "scopePath" in wire ? validateReadableBusPath(wire.scopePath, `${itemPath}.scopePath`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
          itemPath: "itemPath" in wire ? validateBindingPath(wire.itemPath, `${itemPath}.itemPath`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
          role,
          componentType: normalizeDataWireComponentType(wire.componentType || wire.type, role),
          label: "label" in wire ? assertString(wire.label, `${itemPath}.label`, { allowEmpty: true }) : dataPathLabel(sourcePath)
        };
      });
    }

    function validateContextIdentifier(value, path, { fallback = "item" } = {}) {

    function validateDataSource(value, path, { fallbackTarget = "fetch" } = {}) {
      if (value === undefined) return normalizeDataSource({ target: fallbackTarget }, fallbackTarget);
      assertPlainObject(value, path);

        timeoutMs: "timeoutMs" in value ? clamp(assertFiniteNumber(value.timeoutMs, `${path}.timeoutMs`), 1000, 30000) : 8000,
        retries: "retries" in value ? clamp(Math.round(assertFiniteNumber(value.retries, `${path}.retries`)), 0, 5) : 2
      };

    function stateDataAtComponentPath(states, state, component, componentPath) {
      const dataPath = String(component.dataPath || "").trim();
      if (!dataPath) return null;
      const owner = states.find(candidate => {
        const scope = stateDataScopeForId(candidate.id);
        return dataPath === scope || dataPath.startsWith(scope + ".");
      });
      if (!owner) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.dataPath must reference declared state data.`);
      const scope = stateDataScopeForId(owner.id);
      const localPath = dataPath === scope ? "" : dataPath.slice(scope.length + 1);
      const value = localPath ? dataObjectValueAtPath(owner.data, localPath) : owner.data;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.dataPath must reference an object.`);
      return { value, path: `${componentPath}.data` };
    }

    function validateTransitionActionBindings(states, transitions) {
      const transitionById = new Map(transitions.map(transition => [transition.id, transition]));
      const claim = (state, transitionId, path) => {
        const transition = transitionById.get(transitionId);
        if (!transition) throw new Error(`${path} must reference an existing transition.`);
        const direct = transition.from === state.id;
        const parentExit = Boolean(
          state.parentId &&
          transition.from === state.parentId &&
          transition.groupExitId === state.id
        );
        if (!direct && !parentExit) throw new Error(`${path} must reference an outgoing transition of ${state.id}.`);
      };

      states.forEach((state, stateIndex) => {
        state.components.forEach((component, componentIndex) => {
          const componentPath = `definition.model.states[${stateIndex}].components[${componentIndex}]`;
          if (component.type === "transitionButton") {
            const transitionId = String(component.transitionId || "").trim();
            if (!transitionId) throw new Error(`${componentPath}.transitionId must not be empty.`);
            claim(state, transitionId, `${componentPath}.transitionId`);
            return;
          }
          if (component.type !== "daisy") return;
          const scopedData = stateDataAtComponentPath(states, state, component, componentPath);
          if (!scopedData) return;
          collectTransitionBindingEntries(scopedData.value, scopedData.path)
            .forEach(entry => claim(state, entry.transitionId, entry.path));
        });
      });
    }

    function validateBoundaryDefinition(value, path) {
      if (value === undefined) return normalizeBoundaryConfig(null);
      assertPlainObject(value, path);
      const allowed = new Set(["entryId", "exitId", "entryDisabled", "exitDisabled", "title", "note"]);
      for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
        if (!allowed.has(key)) throw new Error(`${path}.${key} is not part of the canonical boundary contract.`);
      }
      for (const key of ["entryDisabled", "exitDisabled"]) {
        if (key in value && typeof value[key] !== "boolean") throw new Error(`${path}.${key} must be a boolean.`);
      }
      return {
        entryId: "entryId" in value ? assertString(value.entryId, `${path}.entryId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        exitId: "exitId" in value ? assertString(value.exitId, `${path}.exitId`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        entryDisabled: Boolean(value.entryDisabled),
        exitDisabled: Boolean(value.exitDisabled),
        title: "title" in value ? assertString(value.title, `${path}.title`, { allowEmpty: true }) : "",
        note: "note" in value ? assertString(value.note, `${path}.note`, { allowEmpty: true }) : ""
      };
    }

    function validateStateTemplatesDefinition(value, path = "definition.stateTemplates") {
      if (value === undefined) return [];
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an array.`);
      if (value.length) throw new Error(`${path} is contract-managed; local preset definitions are not supported.`);

    function validateBlueprintDefinition(data) {
      if (data === null || data === undefined) throw new Error("definition must not be null or undefined.");
      assertPlainObject(data, "definition");
      if (data.kind !== DEFINITION_KIND) throw new Error(`definition.kind must be "${DEFINITION_KIND}".`);
      if (data.schemaVersion !== DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION) throw new Error("definition.schemaVersion must be 2.");
      assertString(data.app, "definition.app");
      assertString(data.savedAt, "definition.savedAt");

      const validated = {
        kind: DEFINITION_KIND,
        schemaVersion: DEFINITION_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        app: data.app,
        savedAt: data.savedAt,
        model: validateModelDefinition(data.model),
        stateTemplates: validateStateTemplatesDefinition(data.stateTemplates),
        camera: { x: 32, y: 32, scale: 1 },
        previewCollapsed: false
      };

      if ("camera" in data) {
        assertPlainObject(data.camera, "definition.camera");
        validated.camera = {
          x: assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.x, "definition.camera.x"),
          y: assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.y, "definition.camera.y"),
          scale: clamp(assertFiniteNumber(data.camera.scale, "definition.camera.scale"), 0.25, 2.4)
        };
      }
      if ("previewCollapsed" in data) {
        if (typeof data.previewCollapsed !== "boolean") throw new Error("definition.previewCollapsed must be a boolean.");
        validated.previewCollapsed = data.previewCollapsed;
      }
      return validated;
    }

    function jsLiteral(value) {
      return JSON.stringify(value)
        .replace(/</g, "\\u003c")
        .replace(/>/g, "\\u003e")
        .replace(/&/g, "\\u0026")
        .replace(/\u2028/g, "\\u2028")
        .replace(/\u2029/g, "\\u2029");
    }

    function exportImageCanInline(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text || /^data:image\//i.test(text)) return false;
      if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return false;
      return Boolean(force || looksLikeImagePath(path) || looksLikeImageValue(text));
    }

    async function inlineExportImageUrl(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!exportImageCanInline(text, path, force)) return value;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(IMAGE_INLINE_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",

t;

    function transitionExportSnapshot(t) {
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(t);
      const snapshot = {
        id: String(t.id || ""),
        from: String(t.from || ""),
        to: String(t.to || ""),
        label: String(transitionDisplayLabel(t)),
        condition: String(t.condition || ""),
        triggerType,
        triggerEvent: normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent({ ...t, triggerType }),
        timerMs: normalizeTransitionTimerMs(t.timerMs),
        set: normalizeDataObject(t.set)
      };
      if (t.groupEntryId) snapshot.groupEntryId = String(t.groupEntryId);
      if (t.groupExitId) snapshot.groupExitId = String(t.groupExitId);
      const triggerMatch = normalizeTransitionTriggerMatch(t.triggerMatch);
      if (!transitionTriggerMatchIsEmpty(triggerMatch)) snapshot.triggerMatch = triggerMatch;
      return snapshot;
    }

    function isBoundaryFlowTransition(t) {
      return Boolean(t?.boundaryFlow) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.from) || isBoundaryProxyId(t?.to);
    }

    function componentExportPayload(type, payload) {
      return {
        kind: COMPONENT_EXPORT_KIND,
        schemaVersion: COMPONENT_EXPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        app: "Zustand",
        exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        component: {
          type,
          ...payload
        }
      };
    }

    function exportStateComponentFile(s) {
      const snapshot = stateExportSnapshot(s);
      const payload = componentExportPayload("state", { state: snapshot });
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      downloadTextFile(
        JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) + "\n",
        `${normalizeId(snapshot.title || snapshot.id)}-${stamp}.state-component.json`,
        "application/json"
      );
    }

    function saveDefinitionFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      const definition = definitionPayload();
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      const text = JSON.stringify(definition, null, 2) + "\n";
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.state.json`, "application/json");
    }

    async function exportHtmlFile() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      persistModel(false);
      saveCamera();
      saveUiState();
      let text;
      try {
        const payload = await inlineExportImages(definitionPayload());
        const appHtml = typeof GENERATED_APP_HTML === "string" ? GENERATED_APP_HTML : await (await fetch(APP_URL, { cache: "no-store" })).text();
        text = buildStandaloneAppHtml(appHtml, payload);
      } catch (err) {
        await modalAlert(err?.message || "Die eigenständige App konnte nicht exportiert werden.", "Export fehlgeschlagen");
        return;
      }
      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
      downloadTextFile(text, `${normalizeId(model.name || "state-app")}-${stamp}.html`, "text/html");
    }

    }

    function normalizeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids) {
      return [...new Set((Array.isArray(ids) ? ids : [ids])

        .filter(id => id && byId(id)))];

    function takeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids = []) {
      const result = new Set(normalizeRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(ids));

    function scheduleSync(reset, opts = {}) {
      if (opts.defaultOverrideStateIds) queueRuntimeDefaultOverrideStateIds(opts.defaultOverrideStateIds);
      clearTimeout(syncTimer);
      if (reset) return syncToApp(true, opts);
      syncTimer = setTimeout(() => syncToApp(false), 40);
    }

    function normalizeId(text) {
      return String(text || "state")
        .trim()
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9_]+/g, "_")
        .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "") || "state";
    }

    function isReservedRuntimeId(id) {
      return /^__runtime(?:_|:)/.test(String(id || ""));
    }

    function normalizeSubscriptionPath(value) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (text === "*") return "*";
      return normalizeContextPath(text, "");
    }

    function normalizeSubscriptions(value) {
      const source = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value || "").split(/[\n,]+/);
      const seen = new Set();
      const result = [];
      source.forEach(item => {
        const path = normalizeSubscriptionPath(item);
        if (!path || seen.has(path)) return;
        seen.add(path);
        result.push(path);
      });
      return result.slice(0, 64);
    }

    function normalizeListItems(items, fallbackText) {
      const allowed = new Set(["text", "link", "image", "note", "divider"]);
      if (Array.isArray(items)) {
        return items
          .filter(item => item && typeof item === "object" && allowed.has(item.type || "text"))
          .map(item => ({
            id: item.id || uid("c"),
            type: item.type || "text",
            text: String(item.text || ""),
            url: String(item.url || "")
          }));
      }
      const lines = String(fallbackText || "").split(/\r?\n/).map(line => line.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      return lines.map(line => ({
        id: uid("c"),
        type: "text",
        text: line,
        url: ""
      }));
    }


    function isDescendant(parentId, childId) {
      let current = byId(childId);
      while (current) {
        if (current.parentId === parentId) return true;
        current = current.parentId ? byId(current.parentId) : null;
      }
      return false;
    }

    function handleBeforeInput(evt) {
      if (evt.inputType !== "historyUndo" && evt.inputType !== "historyRedo") return;
      evt.preventDefault();
      evt.stopImmediatePropagation();
      if (evt.inputType === "historyUndo") undoModel();
      else redoModel();
    }

    function clearInspectorTransitionPulse() {

    }

    function pulseInspectorTransition(edgeId) {
      const id = String(edgeId || "");
      if (inspectorTransitionPulseTimer) clearTimeout(inspectorTransitionPulseTimer);
      inspectorTransitionPulseTimer = 0;
      clearInspectorTransitionPulse();
      if (!id) return false;
      const elements = runtimeEdgePulseElements(id);
      if (!elements.length) return false;

      const variant = inspectorTransitionPulseSerial % 2 ? "a" : "b";
      for (const el of elements) {
        el.dataset.inspectorPulseToken = token;
        el.classList.add("inspector-focus-pulse", `inspector-focus-pulse-${variant}`);
      }
      inspectorTransitionPulseTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        for (const el of runtimeEdgePulseElements(id)) {
          if (el.dataset.inspectorPulseToken !== token) continue;

        } else {
          fitView();
        }
      }
      draw();
      initHistory();
      persistModel();
      syncToApp(true);
    }

    function requestedDemoEntryId() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        const requested = String(url.searchParams.get("demo") || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (["zustand", "website", "demo", "true", "1"].includes(requested)) return "zustand";
        return "";
      } catch (_) {
        return "";
      }
    }

    function consumeDemoEntryUrl() {
      try {
        const url = new URL(window.location.href);
        if (!url.searchParams.has("demo")) return;
        url.searchParams.delete("demo");
        const query = url.searchParams.toString();
        window.history.replaceState(null, "", url.pathname + (query ? "?" + query : "") + url.hash);
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    function demoLabelForId(demoId) {
      return "Digitalisierungsplanung";
    }

    async function exampleEntryModelForId(entryId) {
      return normalizeModel(exampleWebsiteModel());
    }

    function modelMatchesExampleEntry(entryId) {
      if (entryId !== "zustand") return false;
      return JSON.stringify(modelDefinitionValue(model)) === JSON.stringify(modelDefinitionValue(exampleWebsiteModel()));
    }

    async function loadExampleEntryById(entryId, shouldFit = true) {
      const nextModel = await exampleEntryModelForId(entryId);
      loadEditorModel(nextModel, shouldFit);
    }

    function editorAgentServiceUrl(path) {
      const route = String(path || "");
      if (location.hostname === "digitalisierungsplanung.de" || location.hostname === "www.digitalisierungsplanung.de") {
        return "https://realtime.digitalisierungsplanung.de" + route;
      }
      return route;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptUrl() {
      return new URL(editorAgentServiceUrl("/assets/agent-widget.js"), location.href).href;
    }

    function editorAgentScriptContentTypeOk(contentType) {
      const type = String(contentType || "").toLowerCase().split(";")[0].trim();
      return !type || [
        "application/javascript",
        "text/javascript",
        "application/ecmascript",
        "text/ecmascript"
      ].includes(type);
    }

    function editorAgentScriptRegisteredError(scriptUrl) {
      return new Error([
        "KI-Assistent konnte nicht geladen werden.",
        `Script: ${scriptUrl}`,
        "Status: Script geladen, aber dp-agent-widget wurde nicht registriert.",
        "Nächster Schritt: Browser-Konsole auf JavaScript-Fehler prüfen."
      ].join("\n"));
    }

    async function editorAgentLoadError(scriptUrl, originalError = null) {
      const lines = [
        "KI-Assistent konnte nicht geladen werden.",
        `Script: ${scriptUrl}`
      ];
      const controller = typeof AbortController === "function" ? new AbortController() : null;
      const timeout = controller ? setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 4500) : null;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(scriptUrl, {
          method: "GET",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",
          signal: controller?.signal
        });
        const statusText = [response.status, response.statusText].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
        lines.push(`Status: HTTP ${statusText}`);
        const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
        if (contentType) lines.push(`Content-Type: ${contentType}`);
        const corsOrigin = response.headers.get("access-control-allow-origin") || "";
        if (corsOrigin) lines.push(`CORS: ${corsOrigin}`);
        if (!response.ok) {
          lines.push("Nächster Schritt: Realtime-Deploy und Route /assets/agent-widget.js prüfen.");
        } else if (!editorAgentScriptContentTypeOk(contentType)) {
          lines.push("Nächster Schritt: Route liefert kein JavaScript aus.");
        } else {
          lines.push("Script ist erreichbar. Prüfe Browser-Konsole, CSP, Adblocker oder Script-Ausführung.");
        }
      } catch (diagnosticError) {
        lines.push(`Abrufprüfung fehlgeschlagen: ${diagnosticError?.message || String(diagnosticError)}`);
        lines.push("Nächster Schritt: Netzwerk, HTTPS-Zertifikat, DNS oder Origin/CORS prüfen.");
      } finally {
        if (timeout) clearTimeout(timeout);
      }
      if (originalError?.message) lin

===== definition @ 1025040 =====
textContent || "Pausieren";
      });
    }

    function runTopbarAction(action) {

    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", evt => {
      const openMenus = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(".topbar-menu[open]"));
      if (!openMenus.length) return;

      closeTopbarMenus();

        syncTopbarProxyActions();

        actionTarget.click();
      };
    });

    function exportImageCanInline(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!text || /^data:image\//i.test(text)) return false;
      if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(text)) return false;
      return Boolean(force || looksLikeImagePath(path) || looksLikeImageValue(text));
    }

    async function inlineExportImageUrl(value, path, force = false) {
      const text = String(value || "").trim();
      if (!exportImageCanInline(text, path, force)) return value;
      try {
        const response = await fetch(IMAGE_INLINE_URL, {
          method: "POST",
          cache: "no-store",
          credentials: "omit",
          headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ url: text })
        });
        if (!response.ok) return value;
        const payload = await response.json();
        const dataUri = String(payload?.dataUri || "");
        return /^data:image\/[a-z0-9.+-]+;base64,/i.test(dataUri) ? dataUri : value;
      } catch (_) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    async function inlineExportImageValue(value, path = "") {
      if (typeof value === "string") return inlineExportImageUrl(value, path);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const out = [];
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          out.push(await inlineExportImageValue(value[index], path));
        }
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return value;
      const out = {};
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        out[key] = await inlineExportImageValue(child, path ? `${path}.${key}` : key);
      }
      return out;
    }

        for (const component of state.components || []) {
          component.url = await inlineExportImageUrl(
            component.url,
            `${statePath}.components.${component.id || "component"}.url`,
            component.type === "image"
          );
        }
      }
      for (const transition of next.model.transitions || []) {
        transition.set =

    function conditionInputSummary(condition) {
      const inputs = inferConditionInputs(condition);
      if (!inputs.length) return "Keine Eingaben. Immer verfügbar.";
      return "Eingaben: " + inputs.map(input => `${input.name}:${input.type}`).join(", ");
    }

    function inferConditionInputs(condition) {
      const map = new Map();
      const upsert = (name, type = "boolean") => {
        const key = String(name || "");
        if (!key || ["true", "false", "and", "or", "null", "undefined", "always"].includes(key.toLowerCase())) return;
        const previous = map.get(key);
        const rank = { boolean: 0, int: 1, float: 2, text: 3 };
        const nextType = rank[type] > rank[previous?.type || "boolean"] ? type : previous?.type || type;
        map.set(key, { name: key, type: nextType });
      };
      const source = String(condition || "");
      const searchable = source.replace(/(["'])(?:\\.|(?!\1)[\s\S])*?\1/g, "");
      for (const name of searchable.match(/[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*/g) || []) upsert(name);
      for (const numeric of searchable.matchAll(/([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*)\s*(==|!=|>=|<=|>|<)\s*(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/g)) {
        upsert(numeric[1], numeric[3].includes(".") ? "float" : "int");
      }
      for (const quoted of source.matchAll(/([a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(?:\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*)\s*(==|!=)\s*(['"])(.*?)\3/g)) {
        upsert(quoted[1], "text");
      }
      return [...map.values()].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    function valueTypeLabel(value) {
      if (Array.isArray(value)) return "array";
      if (value === null) return "null";
      return typeof value;
    }

    function conditionScopeStates(t) {
      const states = [];
      const seen = new Set();
      const add = state => {
        if (!state || seen.has(state.id)) return;
        seen.add(state.id);
        states.push(state);
      };
      const sourceState = byId(effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(t)) || byId(t.from);
      add(sourceState);
      for (let parent = byId(sourceState?.parentId); parent; parent = byId(parent.parentId)) add(parent);
      add(byId(t.from));
      add(byId(effectiveTransitionTargetStateId(t)) || byId(t.to));
      return states;
    }

    function pathBelongsToState(path, state) {
      const clean = normalizeBindingPath(path, "");
      if (!clean || !state) return false;
      const scope = stateDataScope(state);
      return clean === scope || clean.startsWith(scope + ".");
    }

    function conditionSuggestionScopeRank(t, item) {
      const path = normalizeBindingPath(item?.path || "", "");
      if (!path) return 90;
      const sourceState = byId(effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(t)) || byId(t?.from);
      if (pathBelongsToState(path, sourceState)) return 0;
      for (let parent = byId(sourceState?.parentId); parent; parent = byId(parent.parentId)) {
        if (pathBelongsToState(path, parent)) return 1;
      }
      const fromState = byId(t?.from);
      if (fromState && fromState !== sourceState && pathBelongsToState(path, fromState)) return 1;
      const targetState = byId(effectiveTransitionTargetStateId(t)) || byId(t?.to);
      if (pathBelongsToState(path, targetState)) return 2;
      if (path === "state.current") return 3;
      if (path.startsWith("states.")) return 4;
      if (path.startsWith("events.") || path.startsWith("emitters.") || path.startsWith("realtime.")) return 5;
      return 6;
    }

    function conditionSuggestionSort(t, a, b) {
      const rankDelta = conditionSuggestionScopeRank(t, a) - conditionSuggestionScopeRank(t, b);
      if (rankDelta) return rankDelta;
      return String(a?.path || "").localeCompare(String

    function simpleComponentComposerTemplate(template) {
      const copy = snapshotStateTemplates([template])[0];
      if (!copy) return null;
      const components = normalizeComponents(copy.components);
      if (components.length !== 1 || normalizeDataWires(copy.dataWires).length || components[0].type === "daisy") return copy;
      const component = components[0];
      const wire = (id, sourcePath, role, componentType, label) => ({
        id,
        sourcePath,
        role,
        componentType,
        label
      });
      const apply = (data, dataTypes, dataWires) => {
        copy.components = [];
        copy.data = normalizeStateDataObject(data);
        copy.dataTypes = normalizeDataTypes(dataTypes, copy.data);
        const scope = stateDataScopeForId(copy.rootStateId || copy.id || "");
        copy.dataWires = normalizeDataWires(dataWires.map(item => ({
          ...item,
          sourcePath: scope ? scope + "." + item.sourcePath : item.sourcePath
        })));
        return copy;
      };
      if (component.type === "heading") {
        return apply(
          { title: component.text || "Page title" },
          { title: "text" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "title", "title", "heading", "Title")]
        );
      }
      if (component.type === "text") {
        return apply(
          { text: component.text || "Write the useful explanation here." },
          { text: "text" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "text", "description", "text", "Text")]
        );
      }
      if (component.type === "note") {
        return apply(
          { note: component.text || "Important context goes here." },
          { note: "text" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "note", "note", "note", "Note")]
        );
      }
      if (component.type === "image") {
        return apply(
          { image: { url: component.url || DEFAULT_IMAGE_COMPONENT_URL, alt: component.text || "Image description" } },
          { image: "object", "image.url": "image", "image.alt": "text" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "image", "image", "image", "Image")]
        );
      }
      if (component.type === "link") {
        return apply(
          { link: { label: component.text || "Dokumentation öffnen", url: component.url || "https://example.com/docs" } },
          { link: "object", "link.label": "text", "link.url": "url" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "link", "link", "link", "Link")]
        );
      }
      if (component.type === "list") {
        return apply(
          { items: normalizeListItems(component.items, component.text).map(item => item.text).filter(Boolean) },
          { items: "list" },
          [wire(component.id + "_wire", "items", "description", "text", "Einträge")]
        );
      }
      return copy;
    }

    function composerTemplateFrom(template) {
      const copy = simpleComponentComposerTemplate(template);
      if (!copy) return null;
      copy.title = String(copy.title || template.title || "Zustandsvorlage");
      copy.description = String(template.description || copy.description || "");
      copy.builtIn = Boolean(template.builtIn);
      return copy;
    }

    function composerTemplateScope(template) {
      return stateDataScopeForId(template?.rootStateId || template?.id || "");
    }

    function composerActualPath(template, value) {
      const raw = normalizeDataTypePath(value, "");
      if (!raw) return "";
      const scope = composerTemplateScope(template);
      if (scope && raw.startsWith(scope + ".")) return raw.slice(scope.length + 1);
      if (raw === scope || raw.startsWith("states.")) return "";
      return raw;
    }

    function composerDisplayPath(template, path) {
      return composerActualPath(template, path) || String(path || "");
    }

    function composerHiddenVariablePath(path) {
      const last = String(path || "").split(".").pop() || "";
      return ["clickedAt", "startedAt", "endsAt", "status", "loading", "done", "ok", "error", "statusCode", "count"].includes(last);
    }

    function scalarListValue(value) {
      return Array.isArray(value) && value.every(item => isScalarDataValue(item));

    function normalizeChoiceItems(value) {
      if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];
      return value.map((item, index) => {
        const source = isPlainObject(item) ? { ...item } : {};
        source.label = String(source.label ?? source.text ?? source.title ?? item ?? `Option ${index + 1}`);
        source.checked = Boolean(source.checked);
        delete source.text;
        delete source.title;
        return source;
      });
    }

    function isChoiceItemsList(path, value, data = null) {
      const last = String(path || "").split(".").pop();
      if (last !== "items" || !Array.isArray(value)) return false;
      if (!value.length) {
        const aggregatePath = choiceItemsAggregatePath(path);
        return Boolean(aggregatePath && data && dataObjectHasPath(data, aggregatePath));
      }
      return value.every(item => isPlainObject(item) && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(item, "checked"));
    }

    function choiceItemsChecked(value) {
      return normalizeChoiceItems(value).some(item => Boolean(item.checked));
    }

    function choiceItemsAggregatePath(path) {
      const clean = normalizeDataTypePath(path, "");
      if (!clean) return "";
      const parts = clean.split(".");
      if (parts[parts.length - 1] !== "items") return "";
      parts[parts.length - 1] = "checked";
      return parts.join(".");
    }

    function loadEditorState() {
      try {
        const text = localStorage.getItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!text) return null;
        const data = JSON.parse(text);
        return data && typeof data === "object" && data.model ? data : null;

    function applyPersistedEditorState(data) {
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) return;
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(data.model)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      const nextModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(data.model)));
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(nextModel)) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(EDITOR_STORAGE_KEY); } catch (_) {}
        return false;
      }
      model = nextModel;
      currentLayerId = data.currentLayerId && byId(data.currentLayerId) ? data.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(data.selected || null);
      return true;
    }

    function clampNumber(value, min, max) {

    function loadUiState() {
      const mobilePreviewLayout = isMobileWorkspaceLayout();
      const previewMin = mobilePreviewLayout ? PREVIEW_PANEL_MOBILE_MIN : PREVIEW_PANEL_MIN;
      const fallback = {
        inspectorCollapsed: true,
        previewCollapsed: defaultPreviewCollapsed(),
        stateExplorerCollapsed: false,
        stateExplorerGroup: "websuite-builder",
        mobileWorkspaceView: "canvas",
        mobileSheetTopRatio: MOBILE_SHEET_DEFAULT_TOP_RATIO,
        inspectorWidth: INSPECTOR_PANEL_DEFAULT,
        previewWidth: mobilePreviewLayout ? PREVIEW_PANEL_MOBILE_DEFAULT : PREVIEW_PANEL_DEFAULT,
        stateExplorerHeight: STATE_EXPLORER_DOCK_DEFAULT
      };
      try {
        const stored = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UI_STATE_KEY) || "null");

    function initHistory() {
      historyCurrent = editorSnapshot();

      redoStack = [];

      updateHistoryButtons();

    function restoreRejectedEditorMutation() {
      if (!historyCurrent) throw new Error("A valid editor history snapshot is required before changing the model.");
      const restored = parseEditorSnapshot(historyCurrent);
      const restoredModel = normalizeModel(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(restored.model)));
      const issue = modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(restoredModel);
      if (issue) throw new Error(issue.message);
      model = restoredModel;
      currentLayerId = restored.currentLayerId && byId(restored.currentLayerId) ? restored.currentLayerId : null;
      selected = normalizeSelection(restored.selected);
      connecting = null;
      connectingTargetId = null;
      dragging = null;
      boxSelecting = null;
      updateSelectRect();
      draw();
      renderStateExplorer();
      refreshInspectorForSelection();
    }

    function saveEditorState(historyKey = null, sync = true) {
      if (!flushingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) {
        restoreRejectedEditorMutation();
        return false;
      }
      selected = normalizeSelection();
      const next = editorSnapshot();
      if (next === historyCurrent) {
        persistModel(sync);
        return true;
      }
      const historyGroup = historyKey ? `${historyActionId}:${historyKey}` : null;
      if (!historyCurrent) {
        historyCurrent = next;
      } else if (historyGroup && historyGroup === lastHistoryGroup && undoStack.length) {
        // Repeated mutations within one explicit user interaction remain one undo step.
      } else {
        undoStack.push(historyCurrent);
        if (undoStack.length > 100) undoStack.shift();
      }
      redoStack = [];
      historyCurrent = next;
      lastHistoryGroup = historyGroup;
      persistModel(sync);
      updateHistoryButtons();
      return true;
    }

    function saveModel(historyKey = null) {
      return saveEditorState(historyKey, true);
    }

      return saveEditorState(historyKey, false);
    }

    function undoModel() {
      flushInspectorTextCommit();
      if (!undoStack.length) return;
      const current = editorSnapshot();
      const previous = undoStack.pop();
      redoStack.push(current);
      beginHistoryAction();
      restoreHistorySnapshot(previous);
    }

    function redoModel() {

      undoStack.push(current);

    }

    function isPlainObject(value) {
      return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
    }

    function assertPlainObject(value, path) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be an object.`);
    }

    function assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (typeof value !== "string") throw new Error(`${path} must be a string.`);
      if (!allowEmpty && !value.trim()) throw new Error(`${path} must not be empty.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertFiniteNumber(value, path) {
      if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      return value;
    }

    function assertNoNullish(value, path = "definition") {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) throw new Error(`${path} must not be null or undefined.`);
      if (typeof value === "number" && !Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a finite number.`);
      if (!["boolean", "number", "object", "string"].includes(typeof value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a JSON value.`);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, index)) throw new Error(`${path}[${index}] must not be an array hole.`);
          assertNoNullish(value[index], `${path}[${index}]`);
        }
        return;
      }
      if (typeof value !== "object") return;
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) throw new Error(`${path} must be a plain JSON object.`);
      for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        assertNoNullish(item, `${path}.${key}`);
      }
    }

    function validateNullableString(value, path, { allowEmpty = false } = {}) {
      if (value === null || value === undefined) return "";
      return assertString(value, path, { allowEmpty });
    }

 async function inlineExportImageValue(value, path = "") {
      if (typeof value === "string") return inlineExportImageUrl(value, path);
      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        const out = [];
        for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
          out.push(await inlineExportImageValue(value[index], path));
        }
        return out;
      }
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return value;
      const out = {};
      for (const [key, child] of Object.entries(value)) {
        out[key] = await inlineExportImageValue(child, path ? `${path}.${key}` : key);
      }
      return out;
    }

    async function inlineExportImages(payload) {
      const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(payload));
      for (const state of next.model.states || []) {
        const statePath = `states.${state.id}`;
        state.data = await inlineExportImageValue(state.data, statePath);
        for (const component of state.components || []) {
          component.url = await inlineExportImageUrl(
            component.url,
            `${statePath}.components.${component.id || "component"}.url`,
            component.type === "image"
          );
        }
      }
      for (const transition of next.model.transitions || []) {
        transition.set = await inlineExportImageValue(transition.set, "transition.set");
      }
      return next;
    }

    function downloadTextFile(text, filename, type) {
      const blob = new Blob([text], { type });
      const a = document.createElement("a");
      a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      a.download = filename;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      a.remove();
      setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 1000);
    }

    function transitionExportSnapshot(t) {
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(t);
      const snapshot = {
        id: String(t.id || ""),
        from: String(t.from || ""),
        to: String(t.to || ""),
        label: String(transitionDisplayLabel(t)),
        condition: String(t.condition || ""),
        triggerType,
        triggerEvent: normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent({ ...t, triggerType }),
        timerMs: normalizeTransitionTimerMs(t.timerMs),
        set: normalizeDataObject(t.set)
      };
      if (t.groupEntryId) snapshot.groupEntryId = String(t.groupEntryId);
      if (t.groupExitId) snapshot.groupExitId = String(t.groupExitId);
      const triggerMatch = normalizeTransitionTriggerMatch(t.triggerMatch);
      if (!transitionTriggerMatchIsEmpty(triggerMatch)) snapshot.triggerMatch = triggerMatch;
      return snapshot;
    }
