    function applyTriggerTypeAvailability(select, transition) {
      if (!select || !transition) return;
      for (const option of select.options) {
        const mode = String(option.value || "button");
        const triggerType = mode;
        const triggerEvent = transition.triggerEvent;
        const candidate = {
          ...transition,
          triggerType,
          triggerEvent: ["button", "timer", "auto"].includes(triggerType)
            ? defaultTransitionEvent({ ...transition, triggerType })
            : triggerEvent
        };
        option.disabled = mode !== normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) && modelTransitionTriggerConflict(candidate);
      }
    }

      const proxyPrefix = `proxy:${parentId}:`;
      model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(transition =>
        transition.boundaryFlow?.parentId !== parentId &&
        !String(transition.from || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix) &&
        !String(transition.to || "").startsWith(proxyPrefix)
      );
    }

    function degroupCompositeState(parent) {
      if (!parent || parent.virtualProxy) return false;
      const children = statesInLayer(parent.id);
      if (!children.length) return false;
      const childIds = new Set(children.map(child => child.id));
      const outerParentId = stateParentId(parent);
      const outerParent = outerParentId ? byId(outerParentId) : rootLayerContainer();
      const outerBoundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(outerParent.rootLayer ? model.boundary : outerParent.boundary);
      const entry = childBoundaryState(parent, "input");
      const exit = childBoundaryState(parent, "output");
      if (!entry || !exit) return false;
      const removedLayerIsOpen = Boolean(currentLayerId && (currentLayerId === parent.id || isDescendant(parent.id, currentLayerId)));

      for (const transition of model.transitions) {
        if (isBoundaryFlowTransition(transition)) continue;
        if (transition.to === parent.id) {
          transition.to = childIds.has(transition.groupEntryId) ? transition.groupEntryId : entry.id;
          transition.groupEntryId = "";
        }
        if (transition.from === parent.id) {
          transition.from = childIds.has(transition.groupExitId) ? transition.groupExitId : exit.id;
          transition.groupExitId = "";
        }
        if (transition.to === outerParentId && transition.groupEntryId === parent.id) transition.groupEntryId = entry.id;
        if (transition.from === outerParentId && transition.groupExitId === parent.id) transition.groupExitId = exit.id;
      }

      for (const child of children) {
        child.parentId = outerParentId || null;
      }
      if (outerBoundary.entryId === parent.id) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "input", entry.id);
      if (outerBoundary.exitId === parent.id) setBoundaryEndpoint(outerParent, "output", exit.id);
      if (model.initial === parent.id) model.initial = entry.id;
      removeBoundaryFlowsForParent(parent.id);
      model.states = model.states.filter(state => state.id !== parent.id);
      model.transitions = model.transitions.filter(transition => transition.from !== parent.id && transition.to !== parent.id);
      ensureDefaultBoundaryTransitions(outerParent, statesInLayer(outerParentId));
      normalizeModel(model);
      if (removedLayerIsOpen) currentLayerId = outerParentId && byId(outerParentId) ? outerParentId : null;
      selected = selectionFromParts(children.map(child => child.id), []);
      if (manualDegroupTargetId === parent.id) manualDegroupTargetId = "";
      if (selectionActionDegroupTargetId === parent.id) selectionActionDegroupTargetId = "";
      showEmptyInspector();
      saveModel("group:degroup");
      draw();
      return true;
    }

    function groupOrDegroupSelected() {
      const composite = compositeStateForDegroupId(selectionActionDegroupTargetId) || selectedCompositeStateForDegroup();
      selectionActionDegroupTargetId = "";
      if (composite) {
        degroupCompositeState(composite);
        return;
      }
      collapseSelectedStatesToParent();
    }

    function applyCamera(options = {}) {
      camera.scale = clampScale(camera.scale);
      const tx = Math.round(camera.x);
      const ty = Math.round(camera.y);
      worldEl.style.transform = `translate(${tx}px, ${ty}px) scale(${camera.scale})`;
      const grid = 24 * camera.scale;
      mapEl.style.backgroundSize = `${grid}px ${grid}px, ${grid}px ${grid}px, 100% 100%, 100% 100%`;
      mapEl.style.backgroundPosition = `${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, ${tx % grid}px ${ty % grid}px, 0 0, 0 0`;
      if (options.persist === false) {
        clearTimeout(cameraSaveTimer);
        cameraSaveTimer = null;
      } else {
        scheduleCameraSave();
      }
    }

    function clampScale(value) {
      return clamp(value, 0.25, 2.4);
    }

    function crispScale(value) {
      return Math.round(clampScale(value) * 100) / 100;
    }

    function wheelDeltaPixels(evt) {
      const lineMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 1 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_LINE;
      const pageMode = typeof WheelEvent === "undefined" ? 2 : WheelEvent.DOM_DELTA_PAGE;
      const modeScale = evt.deltaMode === lineMode ? 16 : evt.deltaMode === pageMode ? 240 : 1;
      const deltaY = Number(evt.deltaY);
      return Number.isFinite(deltaY) ? deltaY * modeScale : 0;
    }

    function wheelZoomFactor(evt) {
      const deltaY = clamp(wheelDeltaPixels(evt), -640, 640);
      return Math.pow(2, -deltaY / WHEEL_ZOOM_PIXEL_DENOMINATOR);
    }

    function zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      const before = screenToWorldXY(clientX, clientY);
      camera.scale = clampScale(nextScale);
      camera.x = clientX - r.left - before.x * camera.scale;
      camera.y = clientY - r.top - before.y * camera.scale;
      applyCamera();
    }

    function zoomAtViewportCenter(nextScale) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      zoomAtClientPoint(r.left + r.width / 2, r.top + r.height / 2, nextScale);
    }

    function zoomByFactor(factor, clientX = null, clientY = null) {
      if (clientX === null || clientY === null) {
        zoomAtViewportCenter(camera.scale * factor);
        return;
      }
      zoomAtClientPoint(clientX, clientY, camera.scale * factor);
    }

    function viewportCenterWorld() {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      return {
        x: (r.width / 2 - camera.x) / camera.scale,
        y: (r.height / 2 - camera.y) / camera.scale
      };
    }

    function keepViewportCenteredOn(worldPoint) {
      const r = canvasViewportRect();
      camera.x = Math.round(r.width / 2 - worldPoint.x * camera.scale);
      camera.y = Math.round(r.height / 2 - worldPoint.y * camera.scale);
      applyCamera();
    }

    function togglePreviewCollapsed() {
      const center = viewportCenterWorld();
      previewCollapsed = !previewCollapsed;
      applyPreviewCollapsed();
      requestAnimationFrame(() => keepViewportCenteredOn(center));
      setTimeout(() => keepViewportCenteredOn(center), 210);
    }

    function resetView() {
      camera = { x: 32, y: 32, scale: 1 };
      applyCamera();
    }

    function escapeHtml(text) {

    function refreshRuntimeInteractionState() {

    function prepareRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object" || payload.type !== "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return payload;
      const syncId = String(payload.syncId || ++runtimeModelSyncSequence);
      pendingRuntimeModelSyncId = syncId;
      refreshRuntimeInteractionState();
      return payload.syncId === syncId ? payload : { ...payload, syncId };
    }

    function runtimeSyncAtOrAfter(value, expected) {
      const actualNumber = Number(value);
      const expectedNumber = Number(expected);
      if (Number.isSafeInteger(actualNumber) && Number.isSafeInteger(expectedNumber)) {
        return actualNumber >= expectedNumber;
      }
      return String(value || "") === String(expected || "");
    }

    function queuePendingRuntimePayload(payload) {
      if (!payload || typeof payload !== "object") return;
      payload = prepareRuntimePayload(payload);
      const type = String(payload.type || "");
      if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      } else if (type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") {
        pendingFramePayloads = pendingFramePayloads.filter(item => item?.type !== type);
      }
      pendingFramePayloads.push(payload);
    }

    function pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(payload) {
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_MODEL") return 0;
      if (payload?.type === "STATE_BLUEPRINT_RUNTIME_CONTROL") return 1;
      return 2;
    }

    function flushPendingRuntimePayloads() {
      if (!appFrameReady || !frameEl.contentWindow || !appFrameContainsGeneratedApp()) return false;
      const queue = pendingFramePayloads
        .map((payload, index) => ({ payload, index }))
        .sort((a, b) => pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(a.payload) - pendingRuntimePayloadPriority(b.payload) || a.index - b.index)
        .map(item => item.payload);
      pendingFramePayloads = [];
      for (let index = 0; index < queue.length; index += 1) {
        try {
          postRuntimePayloadToWindow(frameEl.contentWindow, queue[index], appFrameSessionId);
        } catch (_) {
          pendingFramePayloads.unshift(...queue.slice(index));
          return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    }

    function installEditorAgentBridge() {
      if (editorAgentElement) return editorAgentElement;

      widget.setAttribute("data-editor-bridge", "state-blueprint");
      widget.setAttribute("data-disable-launcher", "true");
      widget.addEventListener("dp-agent-editor-snapshot", handleEditorAgentSnapshot);

    function syncTopbarProxyActions() {
      topbarProxyEls.forEach(proxy => {
        const target = document.getElementById(proxy.dataset.topbarProxy || "");
        proxy.disabled = !target || target.disabled;
        proxy.hidden = Boolean(target?.hidden);
        if (target?.classList.contains("runtime-paused")) proxy.classList.add("runtime-paused");
        else proxy.classList.remove("runtime-paused");
        const pressed = target?.getAttribute("aria-pressed");
        if (pressed === "true" || pressed === "false") proxy.setAttribute("aria-pressed", pressed);
        else proxy.removeAttribute("aria-pressed");
        if (target?.id === "btnPause") proxy.textContent = target.textContent || "Pausieren";
      });
    }

    function runTopbarAction(action) {
      closeTopbarMenus();

    async function bootEditor() {

          const ok = await confirmExampleReplacement(demoLabel);
          if (ok) {
            try {
              await loadExampleEntryById(demoEntry, true);
            } catch (error) {
              reportDemoLoadFailure(error);
            }
          }
        }, 0);
      }
    }

    function workspacePanelWidth() {
      return workspaceEl.getBoundingClientRect().width || window.innerWidth || 1280;
    }

    function workspacePanelHeight() {
      return workspaceEl.getBoundingClientRect().height || window.innerHeight || 720;

    function usesStackedPreviewLayout() {
      return Boolean(window.matchMedia?.("(max-width: 900px)")?.matches);
    }

    function mobileCanvasPreviewSideBySide() {
      return Boolean(window.matchMedia?.("(max-width: 899px) and (pointer: coarse) and (orientation: landscape)")?.matches);
    }

    function visibleRectHeight(rect) {
      if (!rect || rect.width <= 0 || rect.height <= 0) return 0;
      const top = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.top));
      const bottom = Math.max(0, Math.min(window.innerHeight || rect.bottom, rect.bottom));
      return Math.max(0, bottom - top);
    }

    function mobilePreviewRuntimeBottomInsetPx() {
      if (!isMobileWorkspaceLayout() || mobileWorkspaceView !== "app") return 0;
      const commandInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileCommandBarEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const tabsInset = visibleRectHeight(mobileTabsEl?.getBoundingClientRect());
      const visualViewportBottom = window.visualViewport
        ? window.visualViewport.offsetTop + window.visualViewport.height
        : window.innerHeight;
      const browserInset = Math.max(0, (window.innerHeight || 0) - visualViewportBottom);
      return Math.max(0, Math.round(commandInset + tabsInset + browserInset + 16));
    }

    function applyMobilePreviewRuntimeInset() {
      const inset = mobilePreviewRuntimeBottomInsetPx();
      workspaceEl.style.setProperty("--mobile-runtime-preview-bottom-inset", inset + "px");
      try {
        const doc = frameEl?.contentDocument;
        if (!doc?.documentElement) return;
        const value = inset + "px";
        doc.documentElement.style.setProperty("--zustand-host-bottom-inset", value);
        doc.body?.style.setProperty("--zustand-host-bottom-inset", value);
      } catch (_) {}
    }

    function mobileWorkspaceAvailableHeight() {
      const topbarBottom = document.querySelector(".topbar")?.getBoundingClientRect().bottom || 0;
      const tabsTop = mobileTabsEl?.getBoundingClientRect().top || window.innerHeight || 720;
      return Math.max(workspacePanelHeight(), tabsTop - topbarBottom);
    }

    function maxMobilePreviewPanelSize() {
      const available = mobileCanvasPreviewSideBySide() ? workspacePanelWidth() : mobileWorkspaceAvailableHeight();
      const max = Math.min(PREVIEW_PANEL_MAX, available - 120);
      return Math.max(PREVIEW_PANEL_MOBILE_MIN, max);
    }

    function maxInspectorPanelWidth() {
      const workspaceWidth = workspacePanelWidth();
      if (isMobileWorkspaceLayout()) return INSPECTOR_PANEL_MAX;
      if (usesStackedPreviewLayout()) {
        return Math.max(INSPECTOR_PANEL_MIN, Math.min(INSPECTOR_PANEL_MAX, workspaceWidth - 260));
      }
      const previewColumn = previewCollapsed ? 86 : previewPanelWidth;
      return Math.max(INSPECTOR_PANEL_MIN, Math.min(INSPECTOR_PANEL_MAX, workspaceWidth - previewColumn - MIN_CANVAS_COLUMN));
    }

    function maxPreviewPanelWidth() {
      if (isMobileWorkspaceLayout()) return maxMobilePreviewPanelSize();
      if (usesStackedPreviewLayout()) return PREVIEW_PANEL_MAX;
      const workspaceWidth = workspacePanelWidth();
      const inspectorColumn = inspectorCollapsed ? 46 : inspectorPanelWidth;
      return Math.max(PREVIEW_PANEL_MIN, Math.min(PREVIEW_PANEL_MAX, workspaceWidth - inspectorColumn - MIN_CANVAS_COLUMN));
    }

    function maxStateExplorerDockHeight() {
      if (isMobileWorkspaceLayout()) return STATE_EXPLORER_DOCK_DEFAULT;
      const mapHeight = mapEl.getBoundingClientRect().height || workspacePanelHeight();
      return Math.max(STATE_EXPLORER_DOCK_MIN, Math.min(STATE_EXPLORER_DOCK_MAX, mapHeight - MIN_CANVAS_ROW));
    }

    function normalizeSelection(value = selected) {
      if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return null;
      if ("nodes" in value || "edges" in value) return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges, { allCurrentLayer: value.allCurrentLayer === true });
      if (value.type === "node") return selectionFromParts([value.id], []);
      if (value.type === "edge") return selectionFromParts([], [value.id]);
      if (value.type === "nodes") return selectionFromParts(value.ids, []);
      if (value.type === "items") return selectionFromParts(value.nodes, value.edges);
      return null;
    }

    function parseEditorSnapshot(snapshot) {
      const data = JSON.parse(snapshot);
      if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || !data.model) throw new Error("Invalid editor history snapshot.");
      return {
        model: data.model,
        selected: data.selected || null,
        currentLayerId: data.currentLayerId || null
      };
    }

    function persistModel(sync = true) {
      if (modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(model)) return false;

      } catch (_) {}

      return true;
    }

    function cancelInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
        pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = null;
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = null;
    }

    function flushInspectorTextCommit() {
      if (!pendingInspectorTextCommit) return true;
      const pending = pendingInspectorTextCommit;
      cancelInspectorTextCommit();
      flushingInspectorTextCommit = true;
      let saved = false;
      try {
        saved = saveEditorState(pending.historyKey, pending.sync);
      } finally {
        flushingInspectorTextCommit = false;
      }
      if (saved && pending.draw) scheduleDraw();
      return saved;
    }

    function scheduleInspectorTextCommit(historyKey, opts = {}) {
      const next = {
        historyKey,
        sync: opts.sync !== false,
        draw: Boolean(opts.draw)
      };
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommit && pendingInspectorTextCommit.historyKey !== historyKey) {
        flushInspectorTextCommit();
      }
      pendingInspectorTextCommit = pendingInspectorTextCommit
        ? {
            historyKey,
            sync: pendingInspectorTextCommit.sync || next.sync,
            draw: pendingInspectorTextCommit.draw || next.draw
          }
        : next;
      if (pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer) clearTimeout(pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer);
      pendingInspectorTextCommitTimer = setTimeout(flushInspectorTextCommit, opts.delay ?? 120);
    }

      const canUndo = Boolean(undoStack.length);

      if (btnCanvasUndo) btnCanvasUndo.disabled = !canUndo;
      if (btnCanvasRedo) btnCanvasRedo.disabled = !canRedo;
    }

    function eventComposedElements(evt) {
      const path = typeof evt?.composedPath === "function" ? evt.composedPath() : [];
      const nodes = path.length ? path : [evt?.target];
      return nodes.filter(node => node?.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE);
    }

    function eventPathMatches(evt, selector) {
      return eventComposedElements(evt).some(element => element.matches?.(selector) || element.closest?.(selector));
    }

    function targetElement(target) {
      return target?.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE ? target.parentElement : target;
    }

    function elementIsHistoryTextEditTarget(element) {
      if (!element?.matches) return false;
      if (element.matches("textarea, [contenteditable='true']")) return true;
      if (!element.matches("input")) return false;
      return !new Set(["button", "checkbox", "color", "file", "hidden", "image", "radio", "range", "reset", "submit"]).has(String(element.type || "text").toLowerCase());
    }

    function historyTextEditEvent(evt) {
      return eventComposedElements(evt).some(elementIsHistoryTextEditTarget);
    }

    function elementIsEditableTarget(element) {
      return Boolean(element?.closest?.("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]"));
    }

    function editableEventTarget(evt) {
      return eventComposedElements(evt).some(elementIsEditableTarget);
    }

    function agentWidgetEventTarget(evt) {
      return eventPathMatches(evt, "dp-agent-widget");
    }

    function agentWidgetHasFocus() {
      return document.activeElement?.matches?.("dp-agent-widget") || false;
    }

    function eventTargetsEditorChrome(evt) {
      return agentWidgetHasFocus() || editableEventTarget(evt) || eventPathMatches(evt, ".state-explorer, .inspector, .modal-backdrop, button, input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]");
    }

        const fieldTypes = isPlainObject(contribution.fieldTypes) ? contribution.fieldTypes : {};
        const fieldSchemas = isPlainObject(contribution.fieldSchemas) ? contribution.fieldSchemas : {};
        for (const field of Array.isArray(contribution.fields) ? contribution.fields : []) {
          const path = normalizeBindingPath(field, "");
          if (!path) continue;
          out.push({ path, source, type: fieldTypes[path] || fieldSchemas[path]?.type || "value", schema: fieldSchemas[path] || null, value: undefined });
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

    function refreshProductContractConsumers() {
      stateExplorerGroup = normalizeStateExplorerGroup(stateExplorerGroup);
      if (typeof refreshInspectorForSelection === "function") refreshInspectorForSelection();
      if (typeof renderStateExplorer === "function") renderStateExplorer();
    }

    function modelRealtimeTriggerEvents(sourceModel = model) {
      const names = new Set();
      (Array.isArray(sourceModel?.transitions) ? sourceModel.transitions : []).forEach(transition => {
        if (normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) !== "realtime") return;
        const name = normalizeTransitionEvent(transition.triggerEvent || "");
        if (name.startsWith("realtime.")) names.add(name);
      });
      return [...names].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
    }

    function normalizeContextPath(path, fallback = "fetch") {
      const text = String(path || "").trim();
      if (!text) return fallback;
      return /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\.[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*)*$/.test(text) ? text : fallback;
    }

    function normalizeBindingPath(path, fallback = "") {
      const text = String(path || "").trim();
      if (!text) return fallback;
      return /^[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*(\.(?:[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]*|\d+))*$/.test(text) ? text : fallback;
    }

    function dataObjectPathSegments(dataObject, path) {
      const key = String(path || "").trim();
      if (!key) return [];
      return key.split(".").filter(Boolean);
    }

    function dataObjectValueAtPath(data, path) {
      const source = normalizeDataObject(data);
      const parts = dataObjectPathSegments(source, path);
      if (!parts.length) return undefined;
      let node = source;
      for (const part of parts) {
        if (!node || typeof node !== "object" || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node, part)) return undefined;
        node = node[part];
      }
      return node;
    }

    function dataObjectHasPath(data, path) {
      return dataObjectValueAtPath(data, path) !== undefined;
    }

    function setDataObjectPath(data, path, value) {
      const source = normalizeDataObject(data);
      const cleanPath = normalizeDataTypePath(path, "");
      const parts = dataObjectPathSegments(source, cleanPath);
      if (!parts.length) return source;
      let node = source;
      parts.forEach((part, index) => {
        if (index === parts.length - 1) {
          node[part] = value;
          return;
        }
        if (!isPlainObject(node[part])) node[part] = {};
        node = node[part];
      });
      return source;
    }

    function deleteDataObjectPath(data, path) {
      const source = normalizeDataObject(data);
      const parts = dataObjectPathSegments(source, path);
      if (!parts.length) return source;
      let node = source;
      for (const part of parts.slice(0, -1)) {
        if (!node || typeof node !== "object" || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(node, part)) return source;
        node = node[part];
      }
      if (node && typeof node === "object") delete node[parts[parts.length - 1]];
      return source;
    }

    function normalizeComponentDataPath(path, fallback = "") {
      const text = String(path || "").trim();
      if (!text) return fallback;
      return normalizeBindingPath(text, fallback);
    }

    function isRuntimeBookkeepingPath(path) {
      const key = String(path || "").trim();
      return key === "lastEvent" ||
        key === "lastEventAt" ||
        key === "lastChangedPath" ||
        key === "lastChangedAt" ||
        key === "events" ||
        key === "state.previous" ||
        key === "state.lastTransition" ||
        key.startsWith("events.");
    }


    function normalizeDataWireRole(value, fallback = "field") {
      const role = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      return DATA_WIRE_ROLES.includes(role) ? role : fallback;
    }

    function normalizeDataWireComponentType(value, role = "field") {
      const type = String(value || "").trim().toLowerCase();
      if (["heading", "text", "image", "link", "note"].includes(type)) return type;
      if (role === "image") return "image";
      if (role === "title") return "heading";
      if (role === "link") return "link";
      if (role === "note") return "note";
      return "text";
    }

    function normalizeDataWire(value) {
      if (!isPlainObject(value)) return null;
      const sourcePath = normalizeBindingPath(value.sourcePath || value.path, "");
      if (!sourcePath) return null;
      const scopePath = normalizeBindingPath(value.scopePath

      if (triggerType === "flow") return "flow.child.entry";
      return "";
    }

    function transitionUsesRealtimeEvent(transition) {
      return normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) === "realtime";
    }

    function normalizeTransitionTriggerEvent(transition) {
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition);
      const rawTriggerEvent = normalizeTransitionEvent(transition?.triggerEvent || "");
      if (triggerType === "change") {
        return /^(button|event|realtime|timer|auto|flow)\./i.test(rawTriggerEvent) ? "" : rawTriggerEvent;
      }
      if (triggerType === "event") {
        return rawTriggerEvent && !/^(button|change|realtime|fetch|timer|auto|flow)\./i.test(rawTriggerEvent)
          ? rawTriggerEvent
          : "";
      }
      if (triggerType === "realtime") {
        return rawTriggerEvent.startsWith("realtime.") ? rawTriggerEvent : "";
      }
      if (triggerType === "api") {
        return /^fetch\.[a-z0-9_.]+\.(success|error)$/.test(rawTriggerEvent) ? rawTriggerEvent : "";
      }
      if (triggerType === "flow") {
        return rawTriggerEvent === "flow.child.entry" ? rawTriggerEvent : "flow.child.entry";
      }
      if (["button", "timer", "auto"].includes(triggerType)) {
        const prefix = triggerType + ".";
        return rawTriggerEvent.toLowerCase().startsWith(prefix)
          ? rawTriggerEvent
          : normalizeTransitionEvent(defaultTransitionEvent({ ...transition, triggerType }));
      }
      return rawTriggerEvent;
    }

    function modelTransitionTriggerConflict(candidate, sourceModel = model) {
      if (!transitionTriggerTypeIsContractual(candidate)) return true;
      const triggerType = normalizeTransitionTriggerType(candidate);
      if (triggerType === "flow") return false;
      const stateId = effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(candidate);
      const siblings = (sourceModel.transitions || []).filter(transition =>
        transition.id !== candidate.id &&
        normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) !== "flow" &&
        effectiveTransitionTriggerStateId(transition) === stateId
      );
      if (triggerType === "auto" || siblings.some(transition => normalizeTransitionTriggerType(transition) === "auto")) {
        return siblings.length > 0;
      }
      const triggerKey = transitionTriggerContractKey(candidate);
      if (!triggerKey) return false;
      const triggerBaseKey = transitionTriggerBaseKey(candidate);
      const matchKey = canonicalTriggerMatchKey(candidate.triggerMatch);
      if (triggerBaseKey && ["change", "event", "realtime", "api"].includes(triggerType)) {
        const eventSiblings = siblings.filter(transition => transitionTriggerBaseKey(transition) === triggerBaseKey);
        if (matchKey === "*" && eventSiblings.length) return true;
        if (matchKey !== "*" && eventSiblings.some(transition => canonicalTriggerMatchKey(transition.triggerMatch) === "*")) return true;
        if (matchKey !== "*" && eventSiblings.some(transition => transitionTriggerMatchesCanOverlap(candidate, transition))) return true;
      }
      return siblings.some(transition => transitionTriggerContractKey(transition) === triggerKey);
    }

    function modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(sourceModel = model) {
      const byState = new Map();
      const forbiddenBoundaryKeys = ["layerTitle", "comment", "entryTriggerType", "entryTriggerEvent"];
      const stateIds = new Set();
      const stateById = new Map();

        const id = typeof state?.id === "string" ? state.id.trim() : "";
        if (!id) return { code: "missing_state_id", message: "Every state must define an ID." };
        if (isReservedRuntimeId(id)) return { code: "reserved_state_id", stateId: id, message: `State ${id} uses the reserved runtime ID namespace.` };
        if (stateIds.has(id)) return { code: "duplicate_state_id", stateId: id, message: `State ID ${id} must be unique.` };
        stateIds.add(id);
        stateById.set(id, state);
      }
      if (stateIds.size && !stateIds.has(String(sourceModel?.initial || ""))) {
        return { code: "missing_initial", message: "Initial state must reference an existing state." };
      }
      for (const [id, state] of stateById) {
        const parentId = state?.parentId == null || state.parentId === "" ? null : String(state.parentId);

          return { code: "invalid_parent", stateId: id, message: `Parent of ${id} must reference another state.` };

          seen.add(nextParentId);
          current = stateById.get(nextParentId);
        }
      }
      for (const state of sourceModel?.states || []) {
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state, "renderMode")) {
          return { code: "invalid_state_render_mode", stateId: state.id, message: `State ${state.id} contains forbidden renderMode.` };

    function modelTransitionTriggerContractIssue(sourceModel = model) {
      const byState = new Map();
      const forbiddenBoundaryKeys = ["layerTitle", "comment", "entryTriggerType", "entryTriggerEvent"];
      const stateIds = new Set();
      const stateById = new Map();
      for (const state of sourceModel?.states || []) {
        const id = typeof state?.id === "string" ? state.id.trim() : "";
        if (!id) return { code: "missing_state_id", message: "Every state must define an ID." };
        if (isReservedRuntimeId(id)) return { code: "reserved_state_id", stateId: id, message: `State ${id} uses the reserved runtime ID namespace.` };
        if (stateIds.has(id)) return { code: "duplicate_state_id", stateId: id, message: `State ID ${id} must be unique.` };
        stateIds.add(id);
        stateById.set(id, state);
      }
      if (stateIds.size && !stateIds.has(String(sourceModel?.initial || ""))) {
        return { code: "missing_initial", message: "Initial state must reference an existing state." };
      }
      for (const [id, state] of stateById) {
        const parentId = state?.parentId == null || state.parentId === "" ? null : String(state.parentId);
        if (parentId && (!stateIds.has(parentId) || parentId === id)) {
          return { code: "invalid_parent", stateId: id, message: `Parent of ${id} must reference another state.` };
        }
        const seen = new Set([id]);
        let current = state;
        while (current?.parentId) {
          const nextParentId = String(current.parentId);
          if (seen.has(nextParentId)) return { code: "cyclic_parent", stateId: id, message: `Parent chain of ${id} must not contain a cycle.` };
          seen.add(nextParentId);
          current = stateById.get(nextParentId);
        }
      }
      for (const state of sourceModel?.states || []) {
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state, "renderMode")) {
          return { code: "invalid_state_render_mode", stateId: state.id, message: `State ${state.id} contains forbidden renderMode.` };
        }
        if (forbiddenBoundaryKeys.some(key => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state?.boundary || {}, key))) {
          return { code: "invalid_boundary_field", stateId: state.id, message: `State ${state.id} contains a non-contract boundary field.` };
        }
        if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state, "data")) {
          try {
            assertNoNullish(state.data, `state.${state.id}.data`);
            validateStateDataValue(state.data, `state.${state.id}.data`);
          } catch (error) {
            return { code: "invalid_state_data_value", stateId: state.id, message: error.message };
          }
        }
        const componentValues = normalizeComponents(state.components).flatMap(component => [
          component.text,
          component.url,
          ...(Array.isArray(component.items) ? component.items.flatMap(item => [item.text, item.url]) : [])
        ]);
        if (componentValues.some(value => /\{\{[\s\S]*?\}\}/.test(String(value || "")))) {
          return { code: "invalid_component_template", stateId: state.id, message: `State ${state.id} must bind data through dataPath or dataWires.` };
        }
        const children = (sourceModel.states || []).filter(candidate => stateParentId(candidate) === state.id);
        const boundary = normalizeBoundaryConfig(state.boundary);
        if (children.length && !boundary.entryDisabled && !children.some(child => child.id === boundary.entryId)) {
          return { code: "missing_boundary_entry", stateId: state.id, message: `Parent ${state.id} needs an exact entry child or entryDisabled.` };
        }
      }
      if (forbiddenBoundaryKeys.some(key => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(sourceModel?.boundary || {}, key))) {
        return { code: "invalid_boundary_field", message: "Root boundary contains a non-contract field." };
      }
      const transitionIds = new Set();
      for (const transition of sourceModel?.transitions || []) {
        if (transition?.boundaryFlow || isBoundaryProxyId(transition?.from) || isBoundaryProxyId(transition?.to)) continue;
